- **court-form-filling**: The California SC-100 (Plaintiff's Claim and ORDER to Go to Small Claims Court) is an **XFA-based PDF form**, not a standard AcroForm. This distinction is critical — most PDF form-filling libraries (fillpdf, pdfrw, PyPDF2's simple field writer) silently fail or corrupt XFA forms. The correct approach is to **extract the XFA datasets XML, modify it directly, then write it back** using `pypdf`. ([EtaYang10th/spark-skills](https://github.com/EtaYang10th/spark-skills/tree/HEAD/spark_skills_gen/skills_gen_result/all_model_pdi/court-form-filling) · ★109)
